<article>
<div class="vc_row wpb_row vc_row-fluid brick_section_feature_gifs-wrapper brick-wrapper"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container col-sm-12"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><section class="brick_section_feature_gifs brick" id="">
<div class="container">
<div class="row brick_headline">
<div class="col-lg-8 offset-lg-2 text-center">
<h2>
                    All the features you need to make a better employee schedule                </h2>
</div>
<div class="col-lg-8 offset-lg-2 text-center">
<p>
                    Whether you have 30 workers or 300, we have the features you need to communicate with your staff, create and share an employee schedule, and get an overview of your business                 </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="row row-eq-height features clearfix">
<div class="col-lg-4 gif-item">
<div class="gif">
<noscript><img alt="Planday Feature" src="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/revenue@2x-1.jpg"/></noscript><img alt="Planday Feature" class="static" height="382" sizes="(max-width: 696px) 100vw, 696px" src="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/revenue@2x-1.jpg" srcset="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/revenue@2x-1.jpg 696w, https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/revenue@2x-1-300x165.jpg 300w, https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/revenue@2x-1-73x40.jpg 73w" width="696"/> <div class="content">
<h4 class="title">
                            See financial reports in the schedule                        </h4>
<p>
                            In the Schedule, you can see hours worked, payroll costs, revenue, the percentage of revenue paid in salaries, and how that aligns with your targets. With this feature, you can instantly see an in-depth report on your business metrics as you build your schedule.                        </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-lg-4 gif-item">
<div class="gif">
<noscript><img alt="Planday Feature" src="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/schedule-template@2x2-1.jpg"/></noscript><img alt="Planday Feature" class="static" height="382" sizes="(max-width: 696px) 100vw, 696px" src="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/schedule-template@2x2-1.jpg" srcset="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/schedule-template@2x2-1.jpg 696w, https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/schedule-template@2x2-1-300x165.jpg 300w, https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/schedule-template@2x2-1-73x40.jpg 73w" width="696"/> <div class="content">
<h4 class="title">
                            Schedule templates                        </h4>
<p>
                            Templates let you very quickly roll out a new schedule. You can create a template based on an existing schedule, or make one from scratch in the template editor. Use the template editor to keep your schedule in draft mode until you’re ready to publish.                        </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-lg-4 gif-item">
<div class="gif">
<noscript><img alt="Planday Feature" src="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/vacation-request@2x1-1.jpg"/></noscript><img alt="Planday Feature" class="static" height="382" sizes="(max-width: 696px) 100vw, 696px" src="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/vacation-request@2x1-1.jpg" srcset="https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/vacation-request@2x1-1.jpg 696w, https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/vacation-request@2x1-1-300x165.jpg 300w, https://www.planday.com/app/uploads/2017/12/vacation-request@2x1-1-73x40.jpg 73w" width="696"/> <div class="content">
<h4 class="title">
                            Manage employee availability easily                        </h4>
<p>
                            Employees can inform their manager when they prefer to work. They can also edit their availability in real-time so that it’s always up to date. This makes for happier employees and less time spent updating the schedule to accommodate requests.<br/>
</p>
</div>
</div>
